सीसकः, तं विहाय॑ नान्यत्किमप्यस्ति क्रामण न क्रामणमिति भावः । खल्विति

            “Varpya वपा TRicAMJI ACHARYA gave mea copy
      of the Ist volume of the Monthly Magazine called Ayurve-
      diya Granthamala, a monthly Medical Sanskrit. Magazine
      edited and published by him. It contains 3 different re-
      prints of MSS which he has secured, one of them Rasabrida-
      yam Tantram is written by Govindicharya, the Guru of the
       celebrated Shankaracharya, who is supposed                     to     have       lived
       about a 1000 years ago. The Magazine is well conceived
       and if kept up to the standard of the Ist Vol. is calculated
       todo immense good to the Ayurvedic Literature of the
       present time. I wish Vaidya J&davaji every success in the
       work he has undertaken. ”

            It affords ne much pleasure
                                           to have gone through the
      pages of your valuable journal
                                       ५ Ayurvediya Grantha 11414"
      and I accord my hearty and
                                    cordial welcome to it as just
      the thing that is needed for the
                                         resuscitation of Ayurvedas’
      lore at present. The valued works,
                                                with which you have
      commenced the journal promise
                                            a bright career to your
     journal, while the beautiful get-up
                                              of the periodical leaves
     nothing to be desired, I have no doub
                                                   t that the journal
     conducted by one of your deep and
                                                    profound erudition
     can be otherwise than an acquisition and help
                                                          to the labours
     of the modern workers in the field of whom
                                                            I have the
     privilege of being included as one.”

         मुंबईैनगरस्थायुवेंद्विद्यालयव्यवस्थापकानां “एल. एम्‌. एन्ड एत्‌ इत्युपाधि-
     धारिणां Sto पोपट sacra’ इति विख्यतानामभिप्रायः--
           My 9888 Varipya JApavagt Tricawtr                  Acufrya, I am
      very much obliged to you for your kindly sending to me the
     issues of your Ayurvediya Granthamala. The Sanskrit Medi-
     cal science is very vast and there are many works which
     are still not published, It is a great service done to the
     profession especially the Aryan Medical practitioners to pub-
     lish these unknown works. Nobody could do it better
     than you as your sound knowledge of the science and the
     earnest wish of yours to publish these works bespeak your
    fitness to take up the work. Medical practitioners and
    students of the science will both find many things new in
    these works which will add a good deal to their knowledge.
    I have recommended these issues to the students of the
    Aryan Medical School before whom you have lectured for
    a period of about two years and who have always been
    thinking of your interesting lectures_on Vagbhatta. 1 wish
    you all success in this your undertaking.
                                                          I remain,
                                                        _ Yours truly,

         From PAanpit M. Duratswamt AIvancarR, Ayurveda
    Bhushan, Certificated Ayurvedic Doctor, Lecturer, Madras,
    8. 1९. ?. A., Medical College, Assistant Physician 8. K. P. D.
    Ayurvedic Hospital.
    DEAR 818,
          In regard to the Medical monthly “ Ayurvediya
    Granthamala” edited and published by you, I am glad to
    perceive that your journal stands unique in the field of
    Medicine and marks the beginning of sincere attempts to
    resuscitate the Ancient Lore which has been thrown into
    oblivion by the unthinking millions. Your journal takes the
    place of a beacon to lead your benighted brethren along
    the forgotten paths and to help them considerably to unravel
    and appreciate fully the profound truths of the Hindu
     science of Medicine. ` While wishing you hearty success in
    your honest efforts, I hope       no consideration shall deter you
    from carrying out fully your desired object, as you would
    thus be doing a service to the public which would be its
    own reward,
           AYURVEDA VILAS,            \           Yours Faithfully,

                    एवं धन्वनि चम्पकस्य सकले संहारहेतावपि
                त्वे सिश्चन्नगतेन तोयद कुतोऽप्याविष्करतो वेधसा ॥”
         सवषामेव भारतवर्षीयायुवेंदारविन्द्मलिन्दानामतीव सांप्रतं संप्रति खकी-
      याधिहोमजन्योत्साह धूमस्तोमेर॒स्पा: कादम्बिन्याः संवधेनमिति              साग्रहमभ्यर्थ-
     यते, अपि चायुर्वेदीयग्रस्थमाला चिरायुष्मती           तत्थकाशकश्व चिरायुभूयोंदित्या-
     शास्ते च
